local g2 = g and g.DEBUG == true or _G and _G.DEBUG == true or false

local function f1(p1, ...)
  if not g2 then
    return
  end

  local akairoV2 = g and g.AkairoV2

  local v12 = { ... }

  for j = 1, #v12 do
    v12[j] = tostring(v12[j])
  end

  if type(print) == "function" then
    print("[Akairo Debug][legacy." .. tostring(p1) .. "] " .. table.concat(v12, " "))
  end
end

local function f4()

  if v17 and type(v18) == "string" and v18 ~= "" then
    return false
  end

  if type(game.JobId) ~= "string" or game.JobId == "" then
    return nil
  end

  local v19 = request or http_request or type(syn) == "table" and syn.request
  local nextPageCursor, v20 = nil, {}

  for k = 1, 20 do
    if not v16.Alive then
      return nil
    end

    local v21 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. tostring(game.PlaceId)
      .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"

    if nextPageCursor then
      v21 = v21 .. "&cursor=" .. httpService:UrlEncode(nextPageCursor)
    end

    local v22, v23 = pcall(function()
      local body

      if type(v19) == "function" then
        local v24 = v19({ Url = v21, Method = "GET" })

        assert(
          type(v24) == "table" and tonumber(v24.StatusCode or v24.statusCode) == 200,
          "Server list request failed"
        )

        body = v24.Body or v24.body
      else
        body = game:HttpGet(v21)
      end

      local jsonDecode = httpService:JSONDecode(body)

      assert(
        type(jsonDecode) == "table" and type(jsonDecode.data) == "table", "Invalid server list"
      )

      return jsonDecode
    end)

    if not v22 then
      f1("server.detect", v23)
      return nil
    end

    for index2, value4 in ipairs(v23.data) do
      if type(value4) == "table" and value4.id == game.JobId then
        return true
      end
    end

    nextPageCursor = v23.nextPageCursor

    if type(nextPageCursor) ~= "string" or nextPageCursor == "" or v20[nextPageCursor] then
      return nil
    end

    v20[nextPageCursor] = true
    task.wait(0.25)
  end

  return nil
end

function v16.Stop()
  if not v16.Alive then
    return
  end

  f1("runtime", "stop requested")
  v16.Alive = false

  if v16.StopNativeAutos then
    v16.StopNativeAutos()
  end

  if v16.StopStarAuto then
    v16.StopStarAuto()
  end

  if v16.RestoreStarPanel then
    v16.RestoreStarPanel()
  end

  if v16.RestoreBreathingRoll then
    v16.RestoreBreathingRoll()
  end

  if v16.RestoreMovement then
    v16.RestoreMovement()
  end

  if v16.RestoreNick then
    v16.RestoreNick()
  end

  if v16.EndFruitTrip then
    v16.EndFruitTrip()
  end

  if v16.RestoreFruitNotifications then
    v16.RestoreFruitNotifications()
  end

  if v16.RestoreTestingNotice then
    v16.RestoreTestingNotice()
  end

  if v16.RestoreTimeChamberUI then
    v16.RestoreTimeChamberUI()
  end

  if v16.RestoreWebhook then
    v16.RestoreWebhook()
  end

  for index4, value11 in ipairs(v16.Jobs) do
  end

  for index5, value12 in ipairs(v16.Connections) do
    value12:Disconnect()
  end

  if v16.Hidden then
    for key5, value13 in pairs(v16.Hidden) do
      if key5.Parent then
        key5.Enabled = value13
      end
    end
  end

  if v16.Window then
    v16.Window.Gui:Destroy()
  end
end

        instance8.TextSize = 22
        instance8.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 35, 75)
        instance8.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 25, 30)

        table.insert(v61, instance8.Activated:Connect(function()
          local v65 = index12 + value21

          if v65 >= 1 and v65 <= #v62 then
            local v66 = table.find(v25[p64], value20)
            local v67 = table.find(v25[p64], v62[v65])
            v25[p64][v66], v25[p64][v67] = v25[p64][v67], v25[p64][v66]
            f30()
          end
        end))
      end
    end
  end

  v16.Controls[p64] = {
    Kind = "table",
    Default = table.clone(v60),
    API = {
      Set = function(p67, p68)
        v25[p64] = p68
        f30()
      end,
      Reload = function(p69, p70)
        v59 = {}
        v60 = {}

        for index14, value22 in ipairs(p70) do
          v59[value22.Value] = value22.Title
          table.insert(v60, value22.Value)
        end

        f30()
      end,
    },
  }

  f9(akairoWindow.Gui.Destroying, function()
    for index15, value23 in ipairs(v61) do
    end
  end)

  f30()

  if p66 then
    local v68 = ""

    f12(p64 .. "View", 0.2, function()
      local v69 = table.concat(v25[p66] or {}, "|") .. ":" .. table.concat(v25[p64], "|")

      if v69 ~= v68 then
        v68 = v69
        f30()
      end
    end)
  end
end

local function f31()
  return (not v16.StarVisit
      or v16.StarVisit.Farming and f6(v28.Scripts.Interface.Stars.IsRolling))
    and v25.AttackEverything
    and not v25.AutoWorldQuest
    and not v28.Data.Gamemode
    and not v16.ModeJoining
    and (not v16.ModeWinner or v16.ModeWinner == "Farm")
end

local function f32()
  local v71 = {}

  for index16, value24 in ipairs({ "Easy", "Medium", "Hard", "Insane", "Boss", "Secret" }) do
    local v72 = {}

    for key6, value25 in pairs(v28.Shared.Enemies.List[v25.World] or {}) do
      if value25.Difficulty == value24 then
        table.insert(v72, key6)
      end
    end

    table.sort(v72)

    table.insert(v71, {
      Title = value24 .. (#v72 > 0 and " · " .. table.concat(v72, ", ") or ""),
      Value = value24,
    })
  end

  return v71
end

local function f33()
  local v74 = {}

  for key7, value26 in pairs(v28.Shared.Enemies.List[v25.World] or {}) do
    table.insert(v74, {
      Title = key7 .. " · " .. tostring(value26.Difficulty or ""),
      Value = key7,
    })
  end

  table.sort(v74, function(p71, p72) return p71.Title < p72.Title end)
  return v74
end

do
  local team = f21("Team")

  do
    local v82 = f22(f21("Evolve/Feed"), "Auto Evolve")

    f24(
      v82, "AutoEvolveAccessories", "Auto Evolve + Equip Accessories", 1,
      "Automatically evolves all accessories with enough matching duplicates and equips the highest rarity per slot. Locked and equipped accessories are never consumed."
    )

    f28(v82, "Accessory Evolve")
    local v83, v84 = nil, 0

    f12("Accessory Evolve", 1, function()
      local accessories = v28.Data.Accessories
      local accessories2 = v28.Shared.Accessories

      if not accessories or not accessories.List then
        f10("Accessory Evolve", "Waiting for inventory")
        return
      end

      if v83 then
        local v85 = accessories.List[v83.ID]

        if v85 and accessories2.GetRarity(v85) ~= v83.Rarity then
          v83 = nil
          v84 = os.clock() + 1
        elseif os.clock() - v83.At >= 10 then
          v83 = nil
          v84 = os.clock() + 5
          f10("Accessory Evolve", "No confirmation · waiting before retry")
          return
        else
          f10("Accessory Evolve", "Waiting for evolution confirmation")
          return
        end
      end

      if not v25.AutoEvolveAccessories then
        f10("Accessory Evolve", "Disabled")
        return
      end

      if os.clock() < v84 then
        return
      end

      local v86 = {}

      for key8, value28 in pairs(accessories.Equipped or {}) do
        v86[value28] = true
      end

      local v87 = {}

      for key9 in pairs(accessories.List) do
        table.insert(v87, key9)
      end

      table.sort(v87, function(p74, p75)
        local locked = v86[p74] and 2 or accessories.List[p74].Locked and 1 or 0
        local locked2 = v86[p75] and 2 or accessories.List[p75].Locked and 1 or 0

        if locked ~= locked2 then
          return locked > locked2
        end

        return tostring(p74) < tostring(p75)
      end)

      local accessories3 = v28.Scripts.Interface.Accessories
      local evolveAll = accessories3 and accessories3.EvolveAll
      local v88 = type(evolveAll) == "function"

      for index18, value29 in ipairs(v87) do
        local v89 = accessories.List[value29]

        if v89 then
          local v90 = accessories2.GetRarity(v89)

          if accessories2.GetNextRarity(v90) then
            local v91 = {}

            for key10, value30 in pairs(accessories.List) do
              if key10 ~= value29 and not v86[key10] and value30.Locked ~= true
                and value30.Name == v89.Name and accessories2.GetRarity(value30) == v90 then
                table.insert(v91, key10)
              end
            end

            table.sort(v91)
            local v92, v93 = accessories2.GetEvolveResult(v90, #v91)

            if v93 and v93 > 0 then
              if v88 then
                if f11("accessory-evolve-all", 5) then
                  f6(evolveAll)
                  f10("Accessory Evolve", "Native Evolve All requested")
                end

                break
              end

              local v94 = {}

              for m = 1, v93 do
                v94[v91[m]] = true
              end

              v83 = { ID = value29, Rarity = v90, At = os.clock() }

              f10(
                "Accessory Evolve", "Evolving " .. v89.Name .. " · " .. v90 .. " → " .. v92
              )

              f7("Accessories", "Evolve", value29, v94)
              return
            end
          end
        end
      end

      local v95 = {}

      local function f35(p76)
        return f6(function() return v28.Utils.Order:Rarity(accessories2.GetRarity(p76)) end)
      end

      for key11, value31 in pairs(accessories.Equipped or {}) do
        if accessories.List[value31] then
          v95[key11] = value31
        end
      end

      for index19, value32 in ipairs(v87) do
        local v96 = accessories.List[value32]
        local v97 = accessories2.List[v96.Name]
        local v98 = v97 and v97.Type

        if v98 and (not v95[v98] or f35(v96) > f35(accessories.List[v95[v98]])) then
          v95[v98] = value32
        end
      end

      for key12, value33 in pairs(v95) do
        if (accessories.Equipped or {})[key12] ~= value33
          and f11("accessory-equip:" .. key12, 3) then
          f7("Accessories", "Equip", value33)
        end
      end

      f10("Accessory Evolve", "Waiting for duplicates · equipping highest rarity per slot")
    end)
  end

  do
    local v99 = f22(team, "Equip Best")

    f24(
      v99, "EquipBestPets", "Auto Equip Best Pets", 1,
      "Uses the game's Equip Best for fighters every 5 seconds. Takes priority over saved mode teams while enabled."
    )

    f28(v99, "Equip Best Pets")

    f12("Equip Best Pets", 1, function()

  end

  local v100 = f22(team, "Equip Team on Gamemode")

  function v16.ResolveContextRule(p77, p78, p79)
    for index20, value34 in ipairs(p77 or { "Weather", "Mode" }) do
      local v101

      if value34 == "Weather" then
        v101 = p78
      elseif value34 == "Mode" then
        v101 = p79
      end

      if v101 ~= nil and v101 ~= false then
        return v101, value34
      end
    end
  end

  function v16.CurrentWeatherName()
    local weather = v28.Shared.Weather
    local v102 = weather and f6(weather.GetState)
    return v102 and v102.Name
  end

  local v103 = {
    { "World", "World" }, { "Dungeon Easy", "Dungeon" }, { "Trial Easy", "Trial" },
    { "Culling Game", "Culling" },
  }

  local function f36()
    local v104 = { { Title = "None", Value = "None" } }

    for key13, value35 in pairs(v28.Data.Fighters.Teams or {}) do
      if type(value35) == "table" and next(value35) then
        table.insert(v104, { Title = "Team #" .. key13, Value = tostring(key13) })
      end
    end

    table.sort(v104, function(p80, p81)
      return (tonumber(p80.Value) or -1) < (tonumber(p81.Value) or -1)
    end)

    return v104
  end

  for index21, value36 in ipairs(v103) do
    f25(v100, "ModeTeam" .. value36[2], value36[1], f36(), false, "None")
  end

  f24(
    v100, "ModeTeams", "Equip Team on Gamemode", 1,
    "Loads a saved game team when entering World or a mode"
  )

  f27(v100, "Refresh Saved Teams", function()
    for index22, value37 in ipairs(v103) do
      v16.Controls["ModeTeam" .. value37[2]].API:Reload(f36())
    end
  end)

  f28(v100, "Mode Teams")

  do
    local v105 = f22(team, "Equip Team by Weather")

    f24(
      v105, "WeatherTeams", "Equip Team by Weather", 1,
      "Uses saved teams by weather according to Team Priority. None falls back to mode. Auto Equip Best must be disabled."
    )

    for key14 in pairs(v28.Shared.Weather.List) do
      f25(v105, "WeatherTeam_" .. key14, key14, f36(), false, "None")
    end

    f27(v105, "Refresh Saved Teams", function()
      for key15 in pairs(v28.Shared.Weather.List) do

  f29(f22(team, "Team Priority"), "TeamRulePriority", "Team Priority", {
    { Value = "Weather", Title = "Weather" }, { Value = "Mode", Title = "Mode" },
  })

  local v106

  f12("Mode Teams", 0.5, function()
    if not v25.ModeTeams and not v25.WeatherTeams then
      v106 = nil
      f10("Mode Teams", "Disabled")
      return
    end

    if v25.EquipBestPets then
      v106 = nil
      f10("Mode Teams", "Paused for Auto Equip Best Pets")
      return
    end

    local gamemode = v28.Data.Gamemode or "World"
    local v107

    for index23, value38 in ipairs(v103) do
      if value38[1] == gamemode then
        v107 = value38[2]
      end
    end

    local modeTeams = v25.ModeTeams and v107 and tonumber(v25["ModeTeam" .. v107])
    local weatherTeams = v25.WeatherTeams and v16.CurrentWeatherName()
    local v108 = weatherTeams and tonumber(v25["WeatherTeam_" .. weatherTeams])

    local function f37(p82)
      local v109 = p82 and v28.Data.Fighters.Teams[p82]
      return v109 and next(v109) and p82 or nil
    end

    local v110
    modeTeams, v110 = v16.ResolveContextRule(v25.TeamRulePriority, f37(v108), f37(modeTeams))

    if v110 == "Weather" then
      gamemode = "Weather · " .. weatherTeams
    end

    if not modeTeams then
      v106 = nil
      f10("Mode Teams", gamemode .. " · no team selected")
      return
    end

    local v111 = v28.Data.Fighters.Teams[modeTeams]

    if not v111 or not next(v111) then
      v106 = nil
      f10("Mode Teams", "Saved team is empty or missing")
      return
    end

    local v112 = gamemode .. ":" .. tostring(v28.Data.GamemodeSession) .. ":" .. modeTeams

    if v112 ~= v106 then
      f7("Fighters", "LoadTeam", modeTeams)
      v106 = v112
      f10("Mode Teams", gamemode .. " · Team #" .. modeTeams .. " requested")
    end
  end)
end

do
  local v113 = f22(f21("Player"), "Movement")

  local function f38(p83, p84, p85, min, max)
    v25[p83] = tostring(p85)

    local addSlider = v113:AddSlider({
      Title = p84,
      Column = 1,
      Min = min,
      Max = max,
      Default = p85,
      Increment = 1,
      Callback = function(value39) v25[p83] = tostring(value39) end,
    })

    addSlider.ValueBox.TextEditable = false
    addSlider.ValueBox.Active = false
    addSlider.Title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 5)
    addSlider.Title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 20)
    addSlider.ValueBox.Parent.Position = UDim2.new(1, -78, 0, 3)
    addSlider.Track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 30)
    addSlider.Track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 24)

    f23(v113, p83, p84, addSlider, tostring(p85), "string")
  end

  f38("PlayerSpeed", "Player Speed", 32, 0, 300)

  f24(v113, "CustomSpeed", "Enable Player Speed")
  f38("PlayerJump", "Jump Height", 15, 0, 200)
  f24(v113, "CustomJump", "Enable Jump Height")
  f38("FlySpeed", "Fly Speed", 60, 1, 300)
  f24(v113, "Fly", "Fly", 2, "WASD to move · Space up · Left Shift down")
  local v114, v115, v116, instance9, instance10, instance11

  local function f39()
    if instance10 then
      instance10:Destroy()
      instance10 = nil
    end

    if instance11 then
      instance11:Destroy()
      instance11 = nil
    end

    if instance9 then
      instance9:Destroy()
      instance9 = nil
    end

    if v116 and v116.Parent then
      v116.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end

    v116 = nil

    if v114 and v114.Parent and v115 then
      v114.AutoRotate = v115.AutoRotate
      v114.PlatformStand = v115.PlatformStand
    end
  end

  local function f40()
    f39()

    if v114 and v114.Parent and v115 then
      v114.WalkSpeed = v115.WalkSpeed
      v114.JumpHeight = v115.JumpHeight
      v114.JumpPower = v115.JumpPower
    end
  end

  v16.RestoreMovement = f40

  f9(runService.Heartbeat, function()
    local character = localPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if humanoid ~= v114 then
      f40()
      v114 = humanoid

      v115 = humanoid and {
        WalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed,
        JumpHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight,
        JumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower,
        AutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate,
        PlatformStand = humanoid.PlatformStand,
      }
    end

    if not humanoid or not humanoidRootPart or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
      f39()
      return
    end

    if v25.CustomSpeed then
      humanoid.WalkSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(v25.PlayerSpeed) or 32, 0, 300)
    elseif v16.SpeedApplied then
      humanoid.WalkSpeed = v115.WalkSpeed
    end

    v16.SpeedApplied = v25.CustomSpeed

    if v25.CustomJump then
      local v117 = math.clamp(tonumber(v25.PlayerJump) or 15, 0, 300)
      humanoid.JumpHeight = v117
      humanoid.JumpPower = math.sqrt(2 * workspace.Gravity * v117)
    elseif v16.JumpApplied then
      humanoid.JumpHeight = v115.JumpHeight
      humanoid.JumpPower = v115.JumpPower
    end

    v16.JumpApplied = v25.CustomJump

    if not v25.Fly then
      if v116 then
        f39()
      end

      return
    end

    if v116 ~= humanoidRootPart then
      f39()
      v116 = humanoidRootPart
      instance9 = Instance.new("Attachment", humanoidRootPart)

      instance10 = Instance.new("LinearVelocity", humanoidRootPart)
      instance10.Attachment0 = instance9
      instance10.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
      instance10.MaxForce = math.huge

      instance11 = Instance.new("AlignOrientation", humanoidRootPart)
      instance11.Attachment0 = instance9
      instance11.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
      instance11.MaxTorque = math.huge
      instance11.Responsiveness = 25
    end

    humanoid.PlatformStand = true
    humanoid.AutoRotate = false

    local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    if not currentCamera then
      return
    end

    local zero = Vector3.zero

    if not userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
      if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
        zero = zero + currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
      end

      if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
        zero = zero - currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
      end

      if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
        zero = zero + currentCamera.CFrame.RightVector
      end

      if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
        zero = zero - currentCamera.CFrame.RightVector
      end

      if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
        zero = zero + Vector3.yAxis
      end

      if userInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
        zero = zero - Vector3.yAxis
      end
    end

    instance10.VectorVelocity = zero.Magnitude > 0

do

  f28(v118, "Deconstruct")

  local function f41()
    local v122 = {}
    local count = 0

    for key17, value43 in pairs(v28.Data.Fighters.List) do
      local v123 = v28.Shared.Fighters.List[value43.Name]

      local rarity = v123 and v123.Rarity
        and v123.Rarity .. (value43.Shiny and "|Shiny" or "|Normal")

      if rarity and f16(v25.DeconstructRarities, rarity) and not value43.Locked
        and not v28.Data.Fighters.Equipped[key17] and not f16(v25.FeedCharacters or {}, key17)
        and f6(v28.Shared.FighterFeed.GetDeconstructReward, value43) then
        v122[key17] = true
        count = count + 1

        if count >= 50 then
          break
        end
      end
    end

    return v122, count
  end

  local v124

  f12("Deconstruct", 0.2, function()
    if v124 then
      local v125 = v124
      local count2 = 0

      for key18 in pairs(v125.Selected) do
        if v28.Data.Fighters.List[key18] then
          count2 = count2 + 1
        end
      end

      if count2 == 0 or os.clock() - v125.At >= 5 then
        if v125.Thread then
          pcall(task.cancel, v125.Thread)
        end

        v124 = nil
        v16.DeconstructRetryAt = os.clock() + (count2 == 0 and 0.1 or 1)

        f10("Deconstruct", count2 == 0 and "Batch confirmed · checking inventory"
          or "Response timed out · refreshing inventory")
      else
        return
      end
    end

    if not v25.AutoDeconstruct then
      f10("Deconstruct", "Disabled")
      return
    end

    if #v25.DeconstructRarities == 0 then
      f10("Deconstruct", "Select rarities to deconstruct")
      return
    end

    if os.clock() < (v16.DeconstructRetryAt or 0) then
      return
    end

    local v126, v127 = f41()

    if v127 == 0 then
      f10("Deconstruct", "Waiting for matching fighters")
      return
    end

    f10("Deconstruct", "Deconstructing " .. v127 .. " fighters")
    local v128 = { At = os.clock(), Selected = v126 }
    v124 = v128

    v128.Thread = task.defer(function()
      local v129, v130 = pcall(f8, "Fighters", "Deconstruct", v126)

      if v124 ~= v128 or not v16.Alive then
        return
      end

      if not v129 or not v130 then
        v124 = nil
        v16.DeconstructRetryAt = os.clock() + 1
        f10("Deconstruct", "Request rejected · refreshing and retrying")
      else
        f10("Deconstruct", "Waiting for inventory confirmation · " .. v127)
      end
    end)

    table.insert(v16.Jobs, v128.Thread)
  end)
end

      local fighters = v28.Shared.Fighters
      local v154 = math.min(p97, fighters.GetFighterMaxLevel(p96, v28.Data))

      if (p96.Level or 1) >= v154 then
        return {}, v154
      end

      local v155 = -(p96.Exp or 0)

      for n = (p96.Level or 1) + 1, v154 do
        local v156 = fighters.GetNeededExpForLevel(n, p96, v28.Data)

        if not v156 or v156 <= 0 then
          return nil, v154

        v155 = v155 + v156

      if not v157 or v157 <= 0 or v157 == math.huge or v157 ~= v157 then

          break
        end

        local v159 = v28.Shared.FighterFeed.GetAvailableAmount(value52, v28.Data)
        local v160 = v28.Shared.Items.List[value52].FeedExp * v157

        if v159 > 0 and v160 > 0 then

          v158[value52] = v161

      end

      return v158, v154

    local function f53(p107, p108, p109)

        v16.Errors = v16.Errors or {}
        v16.Errors.Webhook = p114

        for index42, value69 in ipairs(v190) do
        end

        v190 = {}

      if not v215 then
        return
      end

      end

      if v215.After and os.clock() < v215.After then
        return
      end

      local v216 = request or http_request or syn and syn.request

      if not v216 then
        f56("Executor does not support HTTP requests")
        return
      end

      local v217, v218 = pcall(v216, {
        Url = v215.URL,
        Method = "POST",
        Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
        Body = v215.Body,
        Timeout = 15,
      })

      if v219 >= 200 and v219 < 300 then
        table.remove(v190, 1)

        if v215.Delivered then
          v215.Delivered()
        end

      elseif v219 == 429 then
        local v220, v221 = pcall(httpService.JSONDecode, httpService, v218.Body or "")

        v215.After = os.clock() + math.max(
          2, v220 and type(v221) == "table" and tonumber(v221.retry_after) or 5
        )

        f10("Webhook", "Rate limited · waiting to retry")
      elseif v219 == 0 or v219 >= 500 then
        v215.Attempts = v215.Attempts + 1

        if v215.Attempts >= 3 then
          f55(v215)
          f56("Delivery failed after 3 attempts · HTTP " .. v219)
        else
          v215.After = os.clock() + 5 * v215.Attempts
          f10("Webhook", "Connection failed · retrying")
        end
      else
        f55(v215)
        f56("Discord rejected notification · HTTP " .. v219)
      end

local v223 = { Traits = "AutoTraits", Breathing = "AutoBreathing", Gacha = "RollGacha" }
local v224 = { "Traits", "Breathing", "Gacha" }
local v225, v226 = 0, nil

local function f57(p115)
  if v16[p115 .. "AutoOwned"] then
    f6((p115 == "Gacha" and f17("Scripts.Interface.Gacha.Default")
      or v28.Scripts.Interface[p115 == "Traits" and "Traits" or "Breathings"]).CancelAuto)

    v16[p115 .. "AutoOwned"] = false
  end
end

function v16.StopNativeAutos()
  f57("Traits")
  f57("Breathing")
  f57("Gacha")
end

function v16.SoloRoll(p116)
  for key30, value70 in pairs(v223) do
    if key30 ~= p116 and v25[value70] and (v16.ActivityReady[key30] or 0) > os.clock() then
      return false
    end
  end

  return true
end

local function f58(p117)
  for index43, value71 in ipairs(v224) do
    if value71 == p117 then
      v225 = index43
      break
    end
  end

  v16.ActiveActivity = nil
  v16.ActivityStartedAt = nil
end

local function f59(p118, p119)
  if not v223[p118] then
    return p119
  end

  v16.ActivityReady[p118] = p119 and os.clock() + 7 or nil

  for key31 in pairs(v223) do
    if v16[key31 .. "AutoOwned"]
      and (not v25[v223[key31]] or not v16.SoloRoll(key31) or key31 == p118 and not p119) then
      f57(key31)
    end
  end

  if v226 then
    local v227 = v226

    if v227.Read() ~= v227.Before then
      v227.Confirmed = true
      v227.ReadyAt = v227.ReadyAt or os.clock() + v227.Cooldown
    end

    if v227.Confirmed and os.clock() >= v227.ReadyAt or os.clock() >= v227.Deadline then
      if not v227.Confirmed then
        v16.Errors.RollRotation = "No confirmation from " .. v227.Name
          .. "; retrying on its next turn"
      else
        v16.Errors.RollRotation = nil
      end

      f58(v227.Name)
      v226 = nil
    else
      f10("Rotation", v227.Name .. " · waiting for roll result / animation")
      return false
    end
  end

  local activeActivity = v16.ActiveActivity

  if activeActivity and os.clock() - (v16.ActivityStartedAt or os.clock()) >= 5 then
    f58(activeActivity)
    activeActivity = nil
  end

  if activeActivity
    and (not v25[v223[activeActivity]] or (v16.ActivityReady[activeActivity] or 0) <= os.clock()) then
    activeActivity = nil
  end

  if not activeActivity then
    for i7 = 1, #v224 do
      local v228 = v224[(v225 + i7 - 1) % #v224 + 1]

      if v25[v223[v228]] and (v16.ActivityReady[v228] or 0) > os.clock() then
        activeActivity = v228
        break
      end
    end
  end

  if activeActivity ~= v16.ActiveActivity then
    v16.ActivityStartedAt = activeActivity and os.clock() or nil
  end

  v16.ActiveActivity = activeActivity

  f10("Rotation", activeActivity and "Next roll: " .. activeActivity
    or "Waiting for enabled systems with resources")

  return activeActivity == p118
end

local function f60()
  return "Waiting for turn · " .. tostring(v16.ActiveActivity or "rotation")
end

local function f61(p120, p121, p122, p123, fn)
  local function f62()
    local v229 = f6(v28.Shared.Economy.GetBalance, v28.Data, p122)
    return tostring(v229 and v229.Total) .. "|" .. tostring(fn())
  end

  if v16.SoloRoll(p120) and type(p121.StartAuto) == "function" then
    v16.NativeRollProgress = v16.NativeRollProgress or {}
    local v230 = f62()
    local v231 = v16.NativeRollProgress[p120]

    if not v231 or v231.Value ~= v230 then
      v16.NativeRollProgress[p120] = { Value = v230, At = os.clock() }
    elseif os.clock() - v231.At >= math.max(8, p123 + 5) then
      f57(p120)
      v16.NativeRollProgress[p120] = nil
    end

    if not v16[p120 .. "AutoOwned"] then
      f6(p121.CancelAuto)
      f6(p121.StartAuto)
      v16[p120 .. "AutoOwned"] = true
    end

    return
  end

  f57(p120)
  f6(p121.CancelAuto)

  v226 = {
    Name = p120,
    Read = f62,
    Before = f62(),
    Cooldown = math.max(0.2, p123) + 0.3,
    Deadline = os.clock() + math.max(12, p123 + 8),
  }

  local v232, v233 = pcall(f6, p121.Roll)

  if not v232 then
    v226 = nil
    f58(p120)
    error(v233)
  end
end

do
  local function f63(p124, p125)
    local v234 = true
    local autoStop = v28.Data[p124] and v28.Data[p124].AutoStop or {}

    for key32, value72 in pairs(v28.Shared[p124].List) do
      if p124 == "Traits" then
        local v235 = f16(p125, key32)

        if autoStop[key32] == true ~= v235 then
          v234 = false

          if f11("stop:" .. p124 .. key32, 1) then
            f7(p124, "SetAutoStop", key32, v235)
          end
        end
      else
        for key33 in pairs(value72.Rarities) do
          local v236 = f16(p125, key32 .. "|" .. key33)

          if (autoStop[key32] or {})[key33] == true ~= v236 then
            v234 = false

              f7(p124, "SetAutoStop", key32, key33, v236)

  end

  local function f64(p126)

      v16.TraitAutoProgress = nil

      local v243 = f6(v28.Shared.Traits.Get, v28.Data.Fighters.List[v237])
      local v244 = tostring(v242 and v242.Total) .. "|" .. tostring(v243 and v243.Name)
      local traitAutoProgress = v16.TraitAutoProgress

      local v245 = math.max(
        8, f6(v28.Utils.PlayerStats.TraitsCooldown, v28.Data, localPlayer) + 5
      )

      if not traitAutoProgress or traitAutoProgress.Snapshot ~= v244 then
        v16.TraitAutoProgress = { Snapshot = v244, At = os.clock() }
      elseif os.clock() - traitAutoProgress.At >= v245 then

        v16.TraitAutoProgress = nil
      end

        f6(traits3.CancelAuto)
        f6(traits3.Start)
        f6(traits3.StartAuto)

    f6(traits3.Start)

    f61("Traits", traits3, v28.Shared.Traits.Price, f6(v28.Utils.PlayerStats.TraitsCooldown, v28.Data, localPlayer), function()
      local v246 = v28.Data.Fighters.List[v237]

    if type(readfile) ~= "function" or type(isfile) ~= "function"
      or type(isfolder) ~= "function" or type(makefolder) ~= "function" then

  function v16.LoadStartupConfig()
    local akairoLegacyResume = g.AkairoLegacyResume

      local v321, v322 = pcall(function() return httpService:JSONDecode(akairoLegacyResume) end)

        and math.abs(os.time() - v322.At) < 3600 and type(v322.Settings) == "table" then
        local v323 = v16.PublicServerBlocked == true

        if v323 then
          for key47, value96 in pairs(v16.Controls) do

              v322.Settings[key47] = false

          local v324 = v322.Settings[key48]

            value97.API:Set(v324, true)
          end
        end

          v16.ReconnectBlocked = "Legacy disabled: public server confirmed"

              Text = v16.ReconnectBlocked,
              Duration = 20,
            })
          end)

          return
        end

        f10("Config", "Reconnected · session settings restored")
        return
      end
    end

    if type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(v312) then
      return
    end

    local v325, v326 = pcall(function()
      local jsonDecode2 = httpService:JSONDecode(readfile(v312))
      assert(type(jsonDecode2.Profile) == "string", "Invalid Auto Load profile")
      v25.Profile = jsonDecode2.Profile
      v16.Controls.Profile.API:Set(v25.Profile, true)

      if f81() then
        v25.AutoLoad = true
        v16.Controls.AutoLoad.API:Set(true, true)
      end
    end)

    if not v325 then
      f10("Config", "Auto Load failed · " .. tostring(v326))
    end
  end

local function f83(p157, p158, p159, p160)
  if #p157 == 0 then
    return nil, 0
  end

  local y2 = p157[1].Y
  local v333, v334, huge = nil, -1, math.huge
  local count10 = 0

  local function f84(p161, p162)
    local v335 = y2

    if p160 then
      v335 = p160(p161, v335, p162)
    end

    if not v335 then
      return
    end

    local count11 = 0

    for index59, value105 in ipairs(p157) do
      if (value105.X - p161) ^ 2 + (value105.Y - v335) ^ 2 + (value105.Z - p162) ^ 2
        <= p158 ^ 2 + 0.00001 then
        count11 = count11 + 1
      end
    end

    local v336 = (p159.X - p161) ^ 2 + (p159.Y - v335) ^ 2 + (p159.Z - p162) ^ 2

    if count11 > v334 or count11 == v334 and v336 < huge then
      v333 = { X = p161, Y = v335, Z = p162 }
      v334 = count11
      huge = v336
    end

    count10 = count10 + 1

    if count10 % 128 == 0 and task then
      task.wait()
    end
  end

  f84(p159.X, p159.Z)

  for index60, value106 in ipairs(p157) do
    f84(value106.X, value106.Z)
  end

  for i9 = 1, #p157 do
    local v337 = p157[i9]
    local v338 = p158 ^ 2 - (v337.Y - y2) ^ 2

    if v338 >= 0 then
      for i10 = i9 + 1, #p157 do
        local v339 = p157[i10]
        local v340 = p158 ^ 2 - (v339.Y - y2) ^ 2
        local v341, v342 = v339.X - v337.X, v339.Z - v337.Z
        local v343 = v341 * v341 + v342 * v342

        if v340 >= 0 and v343 > 0.00001 then
          local v344 = math.sqrt(v343)
          local v345, v346 = math.sqrt(v338), math.sqrt(v340)

          if v344 <= v345 + v346 and v344 >= math.abs(v345 - v346) then
            local v347 = (v338 - v340 + v343) / (2 * v344)
            local v348 = math.sqrt(math.max(0, v338 - v347 * v347))
            local v349, v350 = v337.X + v347 * v341 / v344, v337.Z + v347 * v342 / v344

            f84(v349 - v348 * v342 / v344, v350 + v348 * v341 / v344)
            f84(v349 + v348 * v342 / v344, v350 - v348 * v341 / v344)
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  return v333, v334
end

local function f85(p163)
  local findFirstChild4 = workspace.Client.Maps:FindFirstChild(v28.Data.Gamemode
    or v28.Data.Maps.Current or "")

  local humanoid2 = p163.Parent and p163.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

  if not humanoid2 then
    return function() return nil end
  end

  local v351 = p163.Size.Y * 0.5 + humanoid2.HipHeight

  if humanoid2.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
    local leftLeg = p163.Parent:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
    v351 = v351 + (leftLeg and leftLeg.Size.Y or 2)
  end

  local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
  raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include

  raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = findFirstChild4
      and { findFirstChild4, workspace.Terrain }
    or { workspace.Client.Maps, workspace.Terrain }

  raycastParams.RespectCanCollide = true

  return function(p164, p165, p166)
    local vector = Vector3.new(p164, p165 + 4, p166)
    local v352 = p165 - 80

    for i11 = 1, 12 do
      local raycast = workspace:Raycast(
        vector, Vector3.new(0, v352 - vector.Y, 0), raycastParams
      )

      if not raycast then
        return nil
      end

      local instance15 = raycast.Instance
      local v353 = false

      while instance15 and instance15 ~= workspace do
        if instance15:IsA("Model")
          and (instance15:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            or collectionService:HasTag(instance15, "Enemy")
            or collectionService:HasTag(instance15, "Fighter")) then
          v353 = true
          break
        end

        instance15 = instance15.Parent
      end

      if not v353 and raycast.Normal.Y > 0.5 then
        return raycast.Position.Y + v351 + 0.2
      end

      vector = Vector3.new(p164, raycast.Position.Y - 0.1, p166)

      if vector.Y <= v352 then
        return nil
      end
    end
  end
end

do
  local function f86()
    local v354 = {}

    function v354.next(p167, p168)
      if not p167 then
        return "Missing"
      end

      if p168
        and (p168.Claimed
          or p167.MaximumCompletions and (p168.Completions or 0) >= p167.MaximumCompletions) then
        return "Complete"
      end

      if not p168 or not p168.Available then
        return "Collect"
      end

      for index61, value107 in ipairs(p167.Missions or {}) do
        local v355 = tonumber((p168.Missions or {})[index61]) or 0

        if v355 < (value107.Amount or 0) then
          if value107.Type ~= "Kill" or not value107.Name then
            return "Unsupported", value107, v355
          end

          return "Farm", value107, v355
        end
      end

      return "Claim"
    end

    return v354
  end

  v16.QuestLogic = f86()

  function v16.WorldQuestStep()
    v16.QuestTarget = nil

    if not v25.AutoWorldQuest then
      f10("World Quest", "Disabled")
      return
    end

    if v28.Data.Gamemode then
      f10("World Quest", "Paused for gamemode")
      return
    end

    local current2 = v28.Data.Maps.Current
    local main = v28.Shared.Quests.List.Main
    local v356 = main and main.List[current2]
    local main2 = v28.Data.Quests.List.Main
    local v357 = main2 and main2.List[current2]
    local v358, v359, v360 = v16.QuestLogic.next(v356, v357)

    if v358 == "Missing" then
      f10("World Quest", current2 .. " · no main quest available")
      return
    end

    if v358 == "Complete" then
      f10("World Quest", current2 .. " · quest completed")
      return
    end

    if v358 == "Collect" then
      local v361, v362 = f6(v28.Shared.Quests.CanCollectQuest, current2, "Main", v28.Data)
      f10("World Quest", current2 .. " · " .. (v361 and "accepting quest" or tostring(v362)))

      if v361 and f11("worldquest:collect:" .. current2, 3) then
        f7("Quests", "Collect", "Main", current2)
      end
    elseif v358 == "Claim" then
      f10("World Quest", current2 .. " · claiming reward")

      if f11("worldquest:claim:" .. current2, 3) then
        f7("Quests", "Claim", "Main", current2)
      end
    elseif v358 == "Farm" then
      v16.QuestTarget = { World = current2, Enemy = v359.Name }

      f10(
        "World Quest", current2 .. " · " .. v359.Name .. " · " .. v360 .. "/" .. v359.Amount
      )
    else
      f10(
        "World Quest",
        "Objective requires manual action · " .. tostring(v359.Title or v359.Type)
      )
    end
  end
end

  local function f93(p170)

  local function f94()
    return function(p171)
      local v388 = {}

      for index64, value112 in ipairs(p171) do

        if not huge2 or huge2 ~= huge2 then
          huge2 = math.huge
        end

        if huge2 > 0 then
          value112.Health = huge2
          table.insert(v388, value112)
        end
      end

      table.sort(v388, function(p172, p173)
        if p172.Health ~= p173.Health then
          return p172.Health < p173.Health
        end

        if p172.IsChest == true ~= (p173.IsChest == true) then
          return p173.IsChest == true
        end

        return tostring(p172.ID) < tostring(p173.ID)
      end)

      local v389 = v388[1]
      return v388, v389 and tostring(v389.Room or v389.ID), v389 and v389.Health
    end
  end

  v16.DungeonHealthOrder = f94()

  local function f95()
    local v390 = {}
    local gamemode4 = v28.Data.Gamemode
    local findFirstChild6

    if gamemode4 then
      local findFirstChild7 = workspace.Server.Enemies.Gamemodes:FindFirstChild(gamemode4)

      findFirstChild6 = findFirstChild7
        and findFirstChild7:FindFirstChild(v28.Data.GamemodeSession or "")

      if not findFirstChild6 then
        return v390
      end
    end

    for index65, value113 in ipairs(collectionService:GetTagged("Enemy")) do
      local data = value113:FindFirstChild("Data")
      local currentPosition = data and data:FindFirstChild("CurrentPosition")
      local autoWorldQuest = not gamemode4 and v25.AutoWorldQuest

      local questTarget = autoWorldQuest and v16.QuestTarget
        and v16.QuestTarget.World == v28.Data.Maps.Current
        and value113:GetAttribute("EnemyName") == v16.QuestTarget.Enemy

      if (gamemode4 and value113:IsDescendantOf(findFirstChild6)
          or not gamemode4
            and value113:GetAttribute("MapName") == f87()
            and (autoWorldQuest and questTarget
              or not autoWorldQuest
                and (#v25.Difficulty == 0
                  or f16(v25.Difficulty, value113:GetAttribute("Difficulty")))))
        and currentPosition
        and not value113:GetAttribute("Died") then
        table.insert(v390, { Enemy = value113, Position = currentPosition.Value.Position })
      end
    end

    local v391 = f14()

    if v25.PrioritizeEnemies and not v25.AutoWorldQuest and not gamemode4 and #v390 > 0 then
      local huge3 = math.huge

      for index66, value114 in ipairs(v390) do
        huge3 = math.min(huge3, f34(value114.Enemy))
      end

      local v392 = {}

      for index67, value115 in ipairs(v390) do
        if f34(value115.Enemy) == huge3 then
          table.insert(v392, value115)
        end
      end

      v390 = v392
    end

    if v391 then
      table.sort(v390, function(p174, p175)
        return (p174.Position - v391.Position).Magnitude
          < (p175.Position - v391.Position).Magnitude
      end)
    end

    if gamemode4 == "Culling Game" then
      local v393 = {}

      for index68, value116 in ipairs(v390) do
        local health = value116.Enemy.Data:FindFirstChild("Health")

        value116.Health = health and health.Value
        value116.Room = "Culling"
        value116.ID = value116.Enemy:GetAttribute("EnemyID") or tostring(value116.Enemy)

        if value116.Enemy:GetAttribute("Shielded") ~= true then
          table.insert(v393, value116)
        end
      end

      local v394 = v16.DungeonHealthOrder(v393)
      return v394[1] and { v394[1] } or {}
    end

    if gamemode4 == "Dungeon Easy" then
      for index69, value117 in ipairs(v390) do
        local health2 = value117.Enemy.Data:FindFirstChild("Health")

        value117.Health = health2 and health2.Value
        value117.Room = value117.Enemy:GetAttribute("RoomIndex")
        value117.ID = value117.Enemy:GetAttribute("EnemyID") or tostring(value117.Enemy)

        local v395 = v28.Shared.Enemies.StaticModels[value117.Enemy:GetAttribute("EnemyName")]
        value117.IsChest = v395 and v395.Category == "Chests" or false
      end

      local v396 = {}
      v16.DungeonFloorRetry = v16.DungeonFloorRetry or setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
      local dungeonWaitingForFloor = false

      for index70, value118 in ipairs(v390) do
        if value118.Enemy:GetAttribute("Shielded") ~= true then
          if os.clock() >= (v16.DungeonFloorRetry[value118.Enemy] or 0) then
            table.insert(v396, value118)
          else
            dungeonWaitingForFloor = true
          end
        end
      end

      v16.DungeonWaitingForFloor = dungeonWaitingForFloor
      local v397, dungeonHealthRoom = v16.DungeonHealthOrder(v396)

      v16.DungeonTreasureStatus = v397[1] and v397[1].IsChest
          and "Lowest-health target · chest room"
        or nil

      v16.DungeonHealthRoom = dungeonHealthRoom

      return v397[1] and { v397[1] } or {}
    end

    return f93(v390)
  end

  function v16.OpenModeDoor(p176)
    local gamemode5 = v28.Data.Gamemode

    if gamemode5 ~= "Dungeon Easy" and gamemode5 ~= "Trial Easy" then
      return false
    end

    local v398 = f14()

    if not v398 then
      return false
    end

    if v16.DoorSession ~= v28.Data.GamemodeSession then
      v16.DoorSession = v28.Data.GamemodeSession
      v16.DoorAttempts = {}
      v16.DungeonDoorChoices = {}
      v16.DoorIdleSince = nil
      v16.LastDoorPrompt = nil
      v16.DoorBacktrackPrompt = nil
    end

    v16.DoorAttempts = v16.DoorAttempts or {}
    v16.DungeonDoorChoices = v16.DungeonDoorChoices or {}

    local pendingDoor = v16.PendingDoor

    if pendingDoor then
      if pendingDoor.Session ~= v28.Data.GamemodeSession then
        v16.PendingDoor = nil
      elseif not pendingDoor.Prompt.Parent or not pendingDoor.Prompt.Enabled then
        if gamemode5 == "Dungeon Easy" then
          v16.DungeonDoorChoices[pendingDoor.Room] = pendingDoor.Child
        end

        v16.PendingDoor = nil
        v16.DoorBacktrackPrompt = nil

        f10("Doors", "Door prompt closed · checking next room")
      elseif os.clock() - pendingDoor.At < 5 then
        return true
      else
        v16.PendingDoor = nil
        v16.DoorBacktrackPrompt = nil
        v16.DoorAttempts[pendingDoor.Prompt] = os.clock() + 5
        v16.DoorIdleSince = os.clock() - 5

        f10("Doors", "Door still closed after 5 seconds · trying another path")
      end
    end

    local v399 = false

    if gamemode5 == "Dungeon Easy" and p176 then
      v16.DoorIdleSince = v16.DoorIdleSince or os.clock()
      v399 = os.clock() - v16.DoorIdleSince >= 5
    end

    local v400 = false
    local v401, magnitude2, v402, v403, v404, v405, v406

    for index71, value119 in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
      if value119:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and value119.Enabled
        and type(value119:GetAttribute("RoomIndex")) == "number"
        and type(value119:GetAttribute("ChildIndex")) == "number" then
        local parent2 = value119.Parent
        local roomIndex = value119:GetAttribute("RoomIndex")
        local v407 = v16.DungeonDoorChoices[roomIndex]

        local v408 = gamemode5 ~= "Dungeon Easy" or not v407
          or v407 == value119:GetAttribute("ChildIndex")

        local v409 = v16.DoorAttempts[value119]
        local v410 = not v409 or os.clock() >= v409

        if gamemode5 == "Dungeon Easy" and not v408 and parent2 and parent2:IsA("BasePart")
          and v410 then
          local magnitude3 = (parent2.Position - v398.Position).Magnitude

          if not v406 or roomIndex < v406 or roomIndex == v406 and magnitude3 < v405 then
            v404, v405, v406 = value119, magnitude3, roomIndex
          end
        end

        if v399 and value119 ~= v16.LastDoorPrompt and parent2 and parent2:IsA("BasePart")
          and v410 then
          local magnitude4 = (parent2.Position - v398.Position).Magnitude

          if not v403 or magnitude4 < v403 then
            v402 = value119
            v403 = magnitude4
          end
        end

        if v408 and parent2 and parent2:IsA("BasePart") then
          v400 = true
        end

        if v408 and parent2 and parent2:IsA("BasePart") and v410 then
          local magnitude5 = (parent2.Position - v398.Position).Magnitude

          if not magnitude2 or magnitude5 < magnitude2 then
            v401 = value119
            magnitude2 = magnitude5
          end
        end
      end
    end

    if p176 and not v400 and v404 then
      v401, magnitude2 = v404, v405
      v16.DoorBacktrackPrompt = v401
      f10("Doors", "Dead end · returning to room " .. tostring(v406) .. " for another path")
    elseif v402 then
      v401, magnitude2 = v402, v403

      v16.DoorBacktrackPrompt = v401
      v16.DoorIdleSince = os.clock()

      f10("Doors", "No progress for 5 seconds · trying another path")
    end

    local doorBacktrackPrompt = v16.DoorBacktrackPrompt

    if p176 and doorBacktrackPrompt then
      if doorBacktrackPrompt.Enabled and doorBacktrackPrompt.Parent
        and doorBacktrackPrompt.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
        if os.clock() < (v16.DoorAttempts[doorBacktrackPrompt] or 0) then
          return true
        end

        v401 = doorBacktrackPrompt
        magnitude2 = (doorBacktrackPrompt.Parent.Position - v398.Position).Magnitude
      else
        v16.DoorBacktrackPrompt = nil
      end
    end

    if not v401 then
      return p176 and v400
    end

    v16.LastDoorPrompt = v401

    if magnitude2 > v401.MaxActivationDistance then
      if not p176 then
        return false
      end

      local position3 = v401.Parent.Position

      local vector2 = v398.Position - position3
      vector2 = Vector3.new(vector2.X, 0, vector2.Z)

      if vector2.Magnitude < 0.1 then
        vector2 = Vector3.zAxis
      else
        vector2 = vector2.Unit
      end

      local v411 = f85(v398)
      local v412

      for index72, value120 in ipairs({ math.min(3, v401.MaxActivationDistance * 0.5), 1, 0.25 }) do
        for index73, value121 in ipairs({
          0, math.pi / 4, -math.pi / 4, math.pi / 2, -math.pi / 2, math.pi,
        }) do
          local v413 = vector2.X * math.cos(value121) - vector2.Z * math.sin(value121)
          local v414 = vector2.X * math.sin(value121) + vector2.Z * math.cos(value121)
          local v415, v416 = position3.X + v413 * value120, position3.Z + v414 * value120
          local v417 = v411(v415, position3.Y, v416)

          if v417 then
            local vector3 = Vector3.new(v415, v417, v416)

            if (vector3 - position3).Magnitude < v401.MaxActivationDistance - 0.1 then
              v412 = vector3
              break
            end
          end
        end

        if v412 then
          break
        end
      end

      if not v412 then
        v16.DoorAttempts[v401] = os.clock() + 3
        f10("Doors", "No reachable floor in door range · retrying another approach")
        return true
      end

      v398.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
      v398.CFrame = CFrame.new(v412)

      return true
    end

    local roomIndex2, childIndex = v401:GetAttribute("RoomIndex"), v401:GetAttribute("ChildIndex")

    if f11("door:" .. tostring(v28.Data.GamemodeSession) .. ":" .. roomIndex2 .. ":"
      .. childIndex, 1) then
      local gamemodeSession4 = v28.Data.GamemodeSession
      v16.DoorAttempts[v401] = os.clock() + 5
      local v418 = v16.ModeInvoke("Gamemodes", "OpenDoor", roomIndex2, childIndex)

      if v28.Data.GamemodeSession ~= gamemodeSession4 then
        return true
      end

      if v418 == true then
        v16.PendingDoor = {
          Prompt = v401,
          Room = roomIndex2,
          Child = childIndex,
          Session = gamemodeSession4,
          At = os.clock(),
        }

        f10("Doors", "Request accepted · waiting for door state change")
      else
        f10("Doors", "Door opening not confirmed · retrying")
      end

      v16.DoorSettleUntil = os.clock() + 0.8
    end

    return true
  end

  f12("Farm", 0.5, function()

    end

      f10(v423, v16.OpenModeDoor(true) and "Opening next room door"

    local v431

        local vector4 = Vector3.new(
          v421.Position.X - position4.X, 0, v421.Position.Z - position4.Z

          vector4 = Vector3.zAxis
        else
          vector4 = vector4.Unit
        end

        local v432 = math.max(0.75, math.min(2.5, v425 * 0.45))

        for index76, value124 in ipairs({ 0, math.pi / 2, -math.pi / 2, math.pi }) do
          local v433 = vector4.X * math.cos(value124) - vector4.Z * math.sin(value124)
          local v434 = vector4.X * math.sin(value124) + vector4.Z * math.cos(value124)
          local v435, v436 = position4.X + v433 * v432, position4.Z + v434 * v432
          local v437 = v426(v435, position4.Y, v436)

          if v437 and math.abs(v437 - position4.Y) <= math.max(4, v421.Size.Y * 2) then
            v427 = { X = v435, Y = v437, Z = v436 }
            v428 = 1
            break
          end
        end
      else
        local v438 = v426(position4.X, position4.Y, position4.Z)

        if v438 then
          v427 = { X = position4.X, Y = v438, Z = position4.Z }
          v428 = 1

        end
      end
    end

      v16.RetreatRequest = v439

      v439.Thread = task.defer(function()

          v28.Signal:Invoke("General", "Combat", "FighterRetreat")

          v16.RetreatBusy = false
          v16.RetreatRequest = nil
        end

        if not v440 then
          v16.Errors.Retreat = tostring(v441)
        end
      end)

      table.insert(v16.Jobs, v439.Thread)

      table.insert(v16.Jobs, task.delay(3, function()
        if v16.RetreatRequest == v439 then
          pcall(task.cancel, v439.Thread)

          v16.RetreatRequest = nil
          v16.RetreatBusy = false
          v16.Errors.Retreat = "Retreat timed out · combat resumed"
        end
      end))
    end

      or string.format("Spot / Range %.1f · %d/%d enemies", v425, v428, #v422)

      v442 = v16.DungeonTreasureStatus .. " · " .. v442
    end

    if v28.Data.Gamemode == "Dungeon Easy" and v16.DungeonHealthRoom then
      v442 = "Lowest HP enemy · room " .. v16.DungeonHealthRoom .. " · " .. v442
    end

    f10("Farm", v442)

    if v423 ~= "Farm" then

    end
  end)

  local v458, v459, v460, position6

  function f98()

        f97()
        position6 = nil
        return

        v448 = os.clock()
        v449 = true
        f97()

      end

        findFirstChild8 = findFirstChild9
          and findFirstChild9:FindFirstChild(gamemodeSession6 or "")
      end

      local function f100(p180)
        local data2 = p180 and p180:FindFirstChild("Data")
        local health3 = data2 and data2:FindFirstChild("Health")

        return p180
          and p180.Parent
          and (v446[p180] or 0) <= os.clock()
          and health3
          and tonumber(health3.Value)
          and tonumber(health3.Value) > 0
          and not p180:GetAttribute("Died")
          and (gamemode6 and findFirstChild8 and p180:IsDescendantOf(findFirstChild8)
            or not gamemode6 and p180:GetAttribute("MapName") == v28.Data.Maps.Current)
      end

      if f100(v455) then
        local currentPosition2 = v455.Data:FindFirstChild("CurrentPosition")

        local magnitude6 = currentPosition2
            and (currentPosition2.Value.Position - v463.Position).Magnitude
          or math.huge

        if magnitude6 > f6(v28.Utils.PlayerStats.AutoAttackRange, v28.Data, localPlayer) then
          for index79, value128 in ipairs(collectionService:GetTagged("Enemy")) do
            local data3 = value128:FindFirstChild("Data")
            local currentPosition3 = data3 and data3:FindFirstChild("CurrentPosition")

            if f100(value128) and currentPosition3
              and (currentPosition3.Value.Position - v463.Position).Magnitude + 2 < magnitude6 then
              f97()
              break
            end
          end
        end
      end

      if f100(v455) and v25.PrioritizeEnemies then
        for index80, value129 in ipairs(collectionService:GetTagged("Enemy")) do
          if f100(value129) and f34(value129) < f34(v455) then
            f97()
            break
          end
        end
      end

      if not f100(v455) then
        f97()
        local huge4, huge5 = math.huge, math.huge
        local v464 = f14()

        for index81, value130 in ipairs(collectionService:GetTagged("Enemy")) do

                and (currentPosition4.Value.Position - v464.Position).Magnitude
              or 0

              huge4 = magnitude7
              huge5 = v465
              v455 = value130
            end
          end
        end

        if v455 then

          if health4 then

              if tonumber(p181) and tonumber(p181) <= 0 then

      end

        v449 = true
        v448 = 0
      end

        v458 = v466

        v457 = true

      end

          end

          end

        end
      end

      end

      f10("Farm", "Attack Everything · " .. tostring(v455:GetAttribute("EnemyName")))
    end)

    v456 = false
    v16.Errors.AttackEverything = not v461 and tostring(v462) or nil

    end
  end
